### 2026-09-10

_Whole file read in four chunks. No CHAT lines in this transcript; echo-candidates has no take_verb / laazem_noun / chat_pairs. Long English-only stretches: 03:40-05:51 (recording / repeat-policy talk), 17:06-18:06 (which verb pair to learn), 46:08-47:58 (when 'I get broken' is used), 57:37-1:02:40 (causative-verb wrap-up). Lesson was a drill on بزعج/بنزعج then كسر/انكسر conjugation tables; most Medi Arabic turns are table recitation. Echoes (engine wrote X = Arabic): 06:47 'alfayn situation' = ألفين ستة وعشرين (Amal 06:54 repeated it back as said, not a fix); 07:20 'Intayn ila tult' = تنتين إلا تلت (his own Latin Arabic); 12:44 'this is take' = probably ضليت repeated (low); 38:33 Amal 'أشياءي نوع الإل' = أشيائي بدون الـ; 43:04 Amal 'شو بدم كمان مرة' = شو بدك؟ كمان مرة; 16:06 Amal 'ليش ما؟' = ليش مع؟; 12:13 Amal 'بدائية' = وردانية (Medi 12:14 repeats وردانية); 13:42 Amal 'بس فعلاً' = بس فعلين; 55:30 Medi 'بفيت' and 57:14 'بزور' are engine garbage (unreadable). Pronunciation only (S4, not written as rows): 03:11 جهزت/جهّزت shadda, 15:02 أزعشتك (ج->ش), 24:12 أزد / 24:25 أزدهم = قصد/قصدهم (ق dropped, Amal confirmed 'أزت yes'), 25:08 بيتايوكي = بيدايقوكي, 26:54 بعيم = بقيم, 27:50 بخسر for بكسر (خ for ك, Amal 'قريب. بكسر' - letter substitution, left as S4), 52:52 اشي/إشي vowel, 09:39 كراجي for كهربجي. Letter-order swaps (زعمت/عزمت, سكرتي/كسرتي, اسكر/اكسر, انسكرت/انكسرت, سكرت/كسرت) ARE written as vocab tier 2 rows per the 09-26 دبا precedent (Amal voiced each fix, Medi at 04:05 himself expects زعمت to be marked wrong); the third reader may rule them S4. Self-fixes left out (GR-22): 06:40 date order (he said 'asha tisa' before Amal 06:42), 13:37 بزعج/بنزعج, 35:17 'لما ولاد' -> 'إحنا كنا ولاد', 35:38 تكرت -> كسرت, 49:31 من الحرارة -> من الشوب, 51:44 بكسور -> بكسر, 10:08 هي -> هو. Not slips: 05:55-06:07 Amal supplied الخميس (he did not know the day of the week, not the word), 22:24 'Is it تكوني or just مبسوطة?' (question), 31:20 'Was it بتكسر?' (question), 36:58 'Is it نكسر?' (question), 44:51 'Is it بنكسر?' (question), 40:30 كاسته vs Amal كاسه (his كاسته = kaasto is the normal 'his cup'; her كاسه is likely the engine's spelling of the same word, no slip written), 12:23 'سكني' colour word unclear (Amal 'nice'). 09:17 Amal taught كهربجي vs مهندس كهربا (new word, written tier 0). 14:28-14:35 and 52:23 are rule explanations, not fixes of a specific line (except where rows say so). / Lesson is Arabic-with-English throughout; long English stretches 03:40-05:51 (recording-rule talk), 17:06-18:06, 46:08-48:00, 57:37-1:02:33 (causative-verb discussion). Amal's lines are Arabic script; no chat lines in this file. Echo: 'Intayn ila tult' (07:20) = تنتين إلا تلت; 'Asha tisa alfayn situation' (06:47) = عشرة تسعة ألفين ستة وعشرين; engine wrote بدائية (12:13) for Amal's وردانية, ليش ما (16:06) for ليش مع, فعلاً (13:42) for فعلين, بتطلّي (32:45) for بتضلي, نوع الإل (38:33) for بدون الـ, كساته (39:58) for كاساته, أزت/أزد (24:12-24:25) for قصد, صحته (42:15) for صح, بفيت (55:30) unreadable, أنا واعي (55:33) unclear. Left out as S4 pronunciation: جهزت -> her وجهّزت (03:13, shadda only), أزعشتك (15:02, ج->ش), بعيم for بقيم (26:54), أجر for إجر (35:45), the dropped ق in بيتايوكي (25:08; the b- part is filed), إشي vowel (52:52). Six letter-order (dyslexia) slips that Amal voiced ARE written as low-confidence vocab-A tier 2 rows (02:49 زعمت, 27:50 بخسر, 29:46 سكرتي, 34:14 إسكر, 43:00 انسكرت, 52:36 سكرت) because Medi at 04:05 himself expects زعمت to be marked wrong - the judge should rule whether S4 excludes them. ### 2026-09-18

_Read all 1030 lines in order. Transcript is mostly Latin-letter Arabic from ~05:00 on (causative/get-X verb drill, prepositions, commands); Arabic script only in the warm-up (00:13-04:48) and scattered words. BIG HOLE 20:17-31:41: the engine dropped nearly all of Medi's and Amal's Arabic words (blank gaps like 'So is it ?', 'Either here', 'Or, or') - the made-me-laugh / tiring / meetings / parents / smile sentences are unreadable; only Amal's chat shows the targets (el-musalsal da77akni, el-shu8ul ta3abni/mut3eb, huwwe beyzahhe2 el-kul, el-6ayyara ta33abatni, el-ijtima3aat beyzah2uni, ahli za33aluni, da77akto). 16:34-19:10 and 21:01-25:30 are English meta discussion (verb-adjectives bizahhe2/bi5awwef/mut3eb). 47:06-48:57 English chat about Farsi. Echoes (engine words = Arabic): 00:17 'يمني، يمني' = mnee7 mnee7 (good); 05:38 'زهرت تك' = zahha2tak; 06:49 'Zehe at' = zhe2at; 09:13 'kuffet' = 5ufet; 49:17 'is it attack?' = ed7ak; 53:21 'is hai, ma is hai hala' = ma tezha2i halla; 1:01:06 'The heck, the heck no' = da77akni (chat el-filem mbaare7 da77akni); 59:21 Amal 'Shutul' = shu kaman?/shu tul?; 16:34 'bizaghe' = beyzahhe2. Not written: 03:04 مختلف->مختلفة self-fix (GR-22); 03:41-04:13 clock time 'tlaat'/'tes3a u 3eshreen' -> Amal 'tentain'/'sitte u 3eshreen' = wrong time of day (fact), not language; 09:13 kuffet->5ufet and 46:13 sawa->sawwa, 53:44/58:42 double-T (tedta3eb, matabina) = pronunciation S4; 12:16 'Hasabit or hasabti' and 55:58-56:11 him/her = questions, not slips; 19:55 kunt->kan self-fixed; 31:36 5awwafthom accepted by Amal ('both right'); 39:45 illi - Amal said 'Illi. Good job' as he produced it, treated as help on the way; 4:27 'beige' appears in her own chat so not tier 3. Names: Zara (brand), Diane, Jolene, Renata/Renad, Japan, Farsi - never glossed. / Large stretches 19:25-31:45 have Medi's and Amal's Arabic words STRIPPED by the engine (lines like 'So is it ?', 'is it or-'), so the work / flight / meetings / parents drills can only be judged from her chat lines; rows 16-19 are low confidence for that reason. Most of the verb drill 05:11-15:52 and 31:17-1:00:07 is Latin-transliterated by the engine (zehet, khuffet, tazahoni ...). 00:17 'يمني يمني الآن' = mnee7 mnee7 halla2 ('الآن' is likely the engine's MSA for halla2, not his word). 04:27 'zara أزرق' = engine for azra2 (Amal's 'أزرق' is a pronunciation echo, not a slip). 09:13 kuffet / 5ufet, 46:13 sawa / sawwa, 53:44 tedta'eb and 58:42 matabina double-T are pronunciation (S4 / F1 / F2), not written. 12:16 'Hasabit or hasabti' and 50:06 'ما تعصبني؟ ما تعصب عليّ' are is-it-X-or-Y questions, not slips. 19:55 kunt -> kan is his own self-fix (GR-22). 44:42 'sakar al jumla' is him mishearing 'shu kul el-jumla' (listening, not a drill). echo: 'is it attack?' (49:17) = اضحك (ed7ak); echo: 'The heck, the heck no' (1:01:06-1:01:08) = ضحكني (da77akni, her chat el-filem mbaare7 da77akni); echo: 'Intaz' (15:23, 15:49) / 'Taz' (53:25) / 'It does.' ### 2026-10-02

_00:00-06:40 is English admin talk about the verb-collocation list (no Arabic practice). 06:40-16:40 free talk (woke up late, lazy morning, trip to uncle, camping). 16:54-1:03:48 is a rule lesson on the tool words awal / taani / aa5er / 8eir / nafs / kul and el-: most Medi turns there are meta-questions about the rule in English or Latin-letter Arabic (17:31-17:53 'awal saa', 57:38-59:49 'awwal marra, tani marra, nafs il ishi'), which I did not score as uses or slips per the brief. Several of Amal's fixes in that block fit no bucket (tool word + el- flips the meaning: awal el-saa3a = beginning of the hour, taani el-saa3a = nothing, 8eir never takes el-, nafs always does) - filed as PROPOSE with one rule line; the after-the-noun adjective fixes (el-saa3a el-oola, el-marra el-a5eera, el-yoam el-awal) are A7/A8. Echo check: echo-candidates has one candidate 14:26 'Forgot it all.' - real English, not an echo. take_verb empty. laazem_noun 09:38 'أنا لازم عطلة' -> Amal 'لازم you should take' = B2 row (GR-27). Engine: 07:02 wrote سمعت - Amal's echo صحيت and chat 's7eet' show he said sme3et for s7eet (counted, GR-24); 07:54 'كان قنيش' is noise around كان; 43:20 engine wrote هذا الثاني - Amal's echo حدا التاني is what he said; 1:04:01 Amal's 'شوفك' is most likely بشوفك with the b- lost by the engine. Not counted: 07:38 على العشرة = repeat of the line Amal just fixed (GR-28); 08:17 في الصباح / 08:20 الصباح - Amal said both are fine; 10:43 أنا خططت self-fix (GR-22); 16:09 تنتين و تلت self-fix; 31:28 'the second car -> the first car' self-fix; 45:35 العشر دقايق he doubted himself but was right; 49:04 'every day' in English then his own 'Is it كل يوم?' before she spoke (self-fix, kul yoam is on her sheet); 51:15 'hadi / hada nafs el-ishi?' is a question about the rule; 59:54 'نفس إشي' Amal's reply ('Or existing. It does.') is unclear, not scored. Pronunciation (S4) left out: 09:23 متشجاع vowel stretch (the word fix شجاع->متشجع is counted). / 00:00-06:40 is an English-only talk about the 410-form verb list (no lesson Arabic apart from أنا بشربه as a sample). 06:44-16:40 free talk (woke up late, plans, date/time/clothes). 16:54-1:03:48 is the tool-word drill (أول / تاني / آخر / غير / نفس / كل) - mostly English meta-talk with short Arabic phrases; many of his Arabic lines there are rule questions ('is it X or Y?'), not uses, and are left out. Engine misses: 07:02 engine wrote أنا سمعت for s7eet (GR-24, written as a row); 07:54 'قنيش' is noise after كان; 10:14 'فقط.' is probably not Arabic he meant; 42:14 Amal 'It's noad' = 'It's good'; 33:42 Amal 'Now.' = 'No.'; 45:05 '3asher y' = Amal saying 'عشر' with a letter; 51:17 Amal 'حدا.' = هادا (confirming hada); 57:25 and 59:16-59:25 'qaid' = غير; 1:04:01 Amal 'شوفك' is her goodbye echo (bashufak), not a correction. ### 2026-10-05

_Whole file read. Long English rule-discussion stretches (15:09-20:20, 40:21-41:58, 43:03-47:17, 59:40-1:03:44). Latin-transliterated Medi Arabic 55:30-1:01:00 and several Amal lines (37:49-38:41). Engine notes: 13:10/13:55 كام على = kaman marra?; 40:21 قبيحة is likely bish3a (Amal 40:24 Why do you not forget bisha, chat bish3a) - no error; 52:17 نفس الـ issue = nafs el-ishi (chat nafs el-ishi); 03:31/03:36 أملكت likely عملت repeated after her prompt; بوع (07:10, 07:56) = bu2a3 with dropped ق (S4, not counted). Self-fixes skipped: 01:20 عندي->كان عندي, 14:09 لا->ما شفت, 14:19 جديد->جديدة, 16:19 مش->ما, 17:43 فكر->بفكر, 22:30 محلي->في محلي, 35:14 بدلي->بدلة, 48:52 يزعج->يزعل, 53:21 إجازة->اجتماع. Not slips: 46:45 يومي الأول (he misunderstood the prompt), 56:32 ru7na vs rawwa7na (she offered Or), 30:54 الشاطئ (MSA; شط not on her sheet). Echo candidates: none are Arabic sound-alikes; take_verb and laazem_noun empty. / Large English/meta stretches (09:07-10:02, 11:41-13:05, 15:20-20:10 quiz on the أغلب rule, 40:24-42:00, 1:03:16-1:03:44). 16:09-17:51 wrong quiz answers about whether أغلب takes ال are rule questions, not filed. 43:00-47:17 is a rule discussion (أول يومي vs يومي الأول). 54:56-1:02:58 he speaks in Latin letters (engine). 07:10 بوع = بقع with dropped ق (S4). 03:31/03:36 أملكت unclear (engine; chat em 5atibti 3emlatha). 22:10-22:37 Amal seemed to mishear (my workers); his في محلي was a self-fix. 48:52-49:03 يزعج then يزعل for 'saddening' (beyza33el) - engine cannot show the doubled ع; not filed.
